# CA — Canada sights: individual worldwide reviews

[Worldwide v2](../../../rules.md) · [Future-country workflow](../../future-country-workflow.md)

Initial inventory only. [UNESCO country list](https://whc.unesco.org/en/statesparties/CA/) and [Parks Canada inscribed list](https://parks.canada.ca/culture/spm-whs/sites-canada) confirm 22 inscribed properties, represented here by individual sites or specifically Canadian components of transboundary properties. Of ten tentative nominations, eight represented by site or Canadian component; Qajartalik and Yukon Ice Patches remain documented research gaps rather than guessed archaeological locations. [Tentative list](https://parks.canada.ca/culture/spm-whs/indicative-tentative). Full serial component inventories (Rockies provincial parks, Tr’ondëk-Klondike, transboundary and Arctic properties) are not exhaustive. No independent fact-check status claimed. Broad official source lists are contextual, not claim-level verification. GPS approximate; Indigenous community access and permits must be respected. Rankings do not guarantee current park access or services.

### Rocky Mountain Parks component coverage
All seven parks now have individual YAML assessments: Banff, Jasper, Yoho, Kootenay, Mount Robson, Mount Assiniboine and Hamber, in addition to the parent property. [Parks Canada confirms the component list](https://parks.canada.ca/culture/spm-whs/sites-canada/sec02h). 

### Tr’ondëk-Klondike component register
[UNESCO](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1564/) names eight: Fort Reliance; Ch’ëdähdëk (Forty Mile); Ch’ëdähdëk Tth’än K’et (Dënezhu Graveyard); Fort Cudahy and Fort Constantine; Tr’ochëk; Dawson City; Jëjik Dhä Dënezhu Kek’it (Moosehide Village); Tthe Zrąy Kek’it (Black City). Dawson City and the parent serial property have records. For the other components, community permissions, ordinary interpreted visitor access and safe representative points are not established; no sensitive coordinates or individual ratings are inferred.

### Pass 221 scope decisions

Churchill polar bear viewing scoped to Wildlife Management Area outside existing Wapusk maternity-den visitor scope, beluga estuary separate; regional coordinate proxies, no guaranteed wildlife. Haida Heritage Centre outside Gwaii Haanas. Rocky Mountaineer separately scoped rail journey; non-additive overlap with parks. Cabot Trail deferred: existing Cape Breton Highlands record explicitly credits sections; West Coast Trail deferred pending parent scope (Parks Canada lists as reserve unit: https://parks.canada.ca/pn-np/bc/pacificrim/activ/ssco-wctu). Existing three unassessed and all prior scores preserved. All high ratings provisional from indexed excerpts, not independently audited; high anchor calibration pending. Canada selected based on low baseline and substantial identified omissions.

### Pass 222 scope decisions

Whistler skiing and gondola grouped, Fathom Five includes Flowerpot Island and wreck viewing, distinct marine designation from Bruce Peninsula. Fort Henry separately scoped component of Rideau Canal World Heritage site, non-additive. Three seasonal festivals separate from permanent sites, no future dates guaranteed. Quebec Winter Carnival distinct from Old Quebec built heritage. Cabot Trail and West Coast Trail earlier parent deferrals retained. All high ratings provisional from actual retrieved indexed excerpts, no independent coordinate or current visitor access verification. Existing three unassessed and prior scores preserved.

### Pass 223 scope decisions

Bowron canoe circuit, Wells Gray waterfalls/lakes, Garibaldi trails, Glacier/Rogers Pass and Grasslands dark sky grouped within respective park scopes. Dempster drive excludes Tombstone park hiking; non-additive geographical overlap. Khutzeymateen permitted northern sanctuary trips distinct from central Great Bear tours; broader ecosystem overlap flagged. Riding Mountain bison herd enclosed. Cross-border White Pass railway deferred pending existing US record scope. No new separate park components, glacier trails or Cabot/West Coast Trail additions. Existing three unassessed and prior scores preserved. Generator classification bug remains deferred. Search excerpts retrieved for all new 90+; no independent coordinate, access or physical condition verification.

### Pass 224 scope decisions

Bonaventure and Percé rock grouped; Witless Bay authorized boat viewing only. East Coast Trail excludes existing Cape Spear and Signal Hill visits, non-additive geographical overlap. Forillon includes adjacent marine excursions, no separate duplicate. Green Gables literary heritage separate from coastal park. Grand Manan/Passamaquoddy whale departures grouped. Stratford and TIFF public performances/screenings, not guaranteed future programmes. Hautes-Gorges seasonal Acropole closure noted. Prior three unassessed and old scores preserved. Actual indexed excerpts retrieved for new 90+ ratings; GPS and current conditions not independently verified. Earlier review entries' evidence labels not independently revalidated; separate evidence audit necessary.

## sight-ca-wag-qaumajuq — Winnipeg Art Gallery and Qaumajuq Inuit Art Centre — 2026-11-27

- YAML: [source](../../../countries/CA/sights/wag-qaumajuq.yaml)

### Pass 225 scope decisions

Qaumajuq integrated with Winnipeg Art Gallery; The Forks excludes human rights museum. Tofino municipal Cox Bay/Chesterman only, excludes Pacific Rim beaches. Lake Superior NMCA separate from Pukaskwa and provincial park. Kluane flightseeing and Alsek rafting deferred as existing park activities (https://parks.canada.ca/pn-np/yt/kluane/activ), cross-border river scope requires separate review. Current exhibition schedules, access and GPS not verified. Existing scores and three unassessed entries preserved. Earlier evidence claims not independently revalidated. ### Pass 228 scope decisions

18 additions, 10 desk-based provisional, 8 source-linked, 6 90+, 36 domestic ranking-row comparisons. CA 214 ranked / 217 total. Saguenay terrestrial trails and via ferrata exclude existing whale watching and marine excursions; Fogo excludes hotel and iceberg trips; Drumheller Hoodoos distinct from Royal Tyrrell and Dinosaur Provincial Park; Naikoon excludes Gwaii Haanas; Canadian Grand Prix race weekend only. Cabot Trail and West Coast Trail deferred due to overlap with Cape Breton Highlands and Pacific Rim respectively. Coordinates approximate and access not verified. All four source-backed nature/event candidates plus other researched candidates scored provisionally, not independently verified. Existing scores and three unassessed retained. Regional screen remains open.

18 additions, 10 desk-based provisional, 8 source-linked, 5 90+, 36 domestic ranking-row comparisons. CA 252 ranked / 255 total. Tumbler Ridge geosites grouped; Kinngait centre excludes private studios; Revelstoke resort excludes national park; Churchill Saskatchewan excludes Manitoba estuary; Fundy Trail excludes Fundy National Park and whole Fundy Footpath; St Martins village caves outside park. Citadelle of Québec deferred pending Old Québec overlap audit (https://www.lacitadelle.qc.ca/en/the-fortress). Kicking Horse vertical superlative omitted due to conflicting operator pages. New coordinates approximate; access and schedules not verified. Existing scores and three unassessed retained. Regional screen remains open.

### Pass 231 scope decisions

18 additions, 12 desk-based provisional, 6 source-linked, 6 90+, 36 domestic ranking-row comparisons. CA 270 ranked / 273 total. Discovery geopark excludes Cape Bonavista lighthouse/adjacent viewpoints and iceberg cruises; Okanagan excludes Myra Canyon; Niagara vineyards exclude historic town and Shaw Festival. Vancouver Celebration of Light indefinitely cancelled (https://vancouver.citynews.ca/2025/11/26/vancouvers-honda-celebration-of-light-fireworks-cancelled-indefinitely/); one-night 2026 replacement not same event (https://vancouver.ca/news-calendar/announcing-summer-lights-in-english-bay-june-2026.aspx). Defer until permanent event status/scope assessed; not a score reduction due to temporary closure. Existing scores and three unassessed retained. New coordinates approximate; current access and schedules not verified. Regional screen remains open.

18 additions, 13 desk-based provisional, 5 source-linked, 5 90+, 36 domestic ranking-row comparisons. CA 306 ranked / 309 total. ### Pass 238 bounded closure decisions

- **West Coast Trail — COVERED:** Pacific Rim review explicitly scores entire reserve; Parks Canada lists trail as reserve unit. No new separate trail score; future component split requires parent recalibration. References: https://parks.canada.ca/pn-np/bc/pacificrim/activ/ssco-wctu
- **Cabot Trail — COVERED / NON-ADDITIVE:** Cape Breton Highlands review explicitly credits Cabot Trail sections and Skyline hiking. Whole 300 km loop would duplicate core park merit; out-of-park scenic segments not separately evaluated. No new umbrella. References: https://parks.canada.ca/pn-np/ns/cbreton/visit
- **Woodland Caribou Provincial Park — COVERED:** Included in Pimachiowin Aki World Heritage property; existing community-based landscape scope. No separate park without parent split. References: https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1415/, https://whc.unesco.org/document/168677
- **White Pass and Yukon Route — COVERED / CROSS-BORDER:** US white-pass-railway review explicitly includes Canadian track segments; no duplicate Canadian departure record. References: ../../../countries/US/sights/white-pass-railway.yaml
- **Kluane flightseeing and Alsek rafting — COVERED / CROSS-BORDER:** Parks Canada lists both as park activities; existing Kluane and Tatshenshini-Alsek scope retained. Full cross-border river route not added. References: https://parks.canada.ca/pn-np/yt/kluane/activ
- **Muskwa-Kechika umbrella — COVERED / NON-ADDITIVE:** Existing Northern Rocky Mountains, Stone Mountain and Muncho Lake parks represent key separate visitor scopes; no duplicate regional umbrella.
- **Celebration of Light — DEFERRED — EVENT IDENTITY:** Indefinite cancellation confirmed in 2026 city council minutes; single-night 2026 replacement is not established as equivalent recurring competition. No penalty for temporary closure; reopen on evidence of restoration or stable successor. References: https://council.vancouver.ca/20260401/documents/pspc20260401minutes.pdf, https://vancouver.ca/news-calendar/announcing-summer-lights-in-english-bay-june-2026.aspx
- **Cliffs of Fundy geopark umbrella — COVERED / NON-ADDITIVE:** Existing Cape Chignecto, Five Islands and other separately scoped Fundy sites remain; no broad umbrella score without boundary and incremental merit review.
- **Full Fundy Footpath — DEFERRED — PARENT SCOPE:** Existing Fundy Trail and Fundy National Park cover major portions; separate through-hike not scored until non-additive scope policy resolved.

5 source-linked provisional additions, 3 90+, 10 domestic ranking-row comparisons. CA 381 ranked / 384 total; +310 since original 74. Bounded discovery queue from passes 221–238 closed, not exhaustive.
